Task ID: 4
Agent: main-agent (Z.ai Code)
Task: Create a Roblox script for the next game — "[💧]Fill Water Tanks" (water tank filling sim, user uploaded water.zip and said "next") — from Template.lua, per GLM_SCRIPTING_RULES.md. User previously requested NO comments in the code (carried forward).

Work Log:
- Extracted water.zip → Template.lua (754 lines, byte-identical to Forest/Golf/Piggy templates via `diff`), "[💧]Fill Water Tanks[Deobf].lua" (40,277 lines), game_dump.txt (14,456 lines).
- Re-read GLM_SCRIPTING_RULES.md; reused established conventions from Golf.lua / Piggy.lua / Forest.lua.
- Verified the networking architecture: game uses **ByteNet** framework, NOT traditional RemoteEvents. Only 3 game remotes exist:
  * ReplicatedStorage.GiveStat → **BAN TRAP** (server: `t.Ban(p1, "Fired trap remote GiveStat")` at line 34374). NEVER fired by the script.
  * ReplicatedStorage.ByteNetReliable (RemoteEvent) — ByteNet reliable channel
  * ReplicatedStorage.ByteNetUnreliable (UnreliableRemoteEvent) — ByteNet unreliable channel
- Verified the ByteNet client API: `Packets = require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Packets)` returns a namespace table; each packet has `.send(args)` (client→server), `.sendTo(args, player)` (server→client), `.listen(callback)` (receive). The script requires the original Packets module directly so it auto-syncs with game updates.
- Verified the COMPLETE packet spec from ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Packets (lines 30401-30830) — 60+ packets with exact struct definitions (bytenet.struct/float32/uint8/string/map/array/bool/nothing). All send calls in the script match these signatures 1:1.
- Verified the server-side fill loop (WaterHandler.startLoop, line 34076): every 0.1s, for each player's plot session, checks `ButtonModelHandler.IsOnButton(player)` (returns `(t3[player] or 0) > 0` — the touch count). If on-button: grants water every 0.3s at `FillRate * multiplier`. If off-button AND water>0: drains `Capacity*0.05` every 0.11s. Drain rate (~45% cap/sec at L1) EXCEEDS fill rate at low tank levels, so sustained touch is critical.
- Verified the fill button touch chain: `Button.Touched` (line 37971, server-side in ButtonModelHandler) increments `t3[player]` and sets `player:SetAttribute("OnFillButton", true)` when count==1; `TouchEnded` decrements and sets false at count==0. The touching part must be a descendant of the plot OWNER's character. `firetouchinterest(HRP, fillButton, 0)` replicates to server's Touched handler.
- Verified the water pipe roll/pickup/place flow: touch RollWaterPipeButton → `RequestRollWaterPipe.send()` (no args) → server responds `WaterPipeRollStarted {pipes:[{slot:uint8, pipeType:string}], rollDuration:float32}` → client picks best slot → `RequestPickupWaterPipe.send({slot:uint8})` → `WaterPipePickedUp {slot}` → touch WaterPipeModel.Hitbox → `RequestPlaceWaterPipe.send()` (no args) → `SyncWaterPipe {hasPipe:bool, pipeType:string}`.

- Verified the CastRoll server→client response: `CastRoll.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5, p6) ... v61 = {token=p1, fish=p2, kg=p3, sc=p4, mut=p5 or "", bite=p6}` — line 36536. So payload = (token, fishName, kg, scale, mutation, biteDurationSeconds).
- Verified the fishing flow (all client-side FishingController closure logic):
  1. Player presses (mouse1/touch/gamepad) → InputBegan sets v39=true, calls startCharge if FishState="idle" → FishState="charging".
  2. Player releases → InputEnded sets v39=false, calls releaseCast if v37="charging" → FishState="casting", after 0.15s delay fires RequestRoll:FireServer(nil).
  3. Server picks fish+kg+mutation+biteDuration, sends back via CastRoll.OnClientEvent → v61 set.
  4. Client waits `tonumber(v61.bite) or 2+random*3` seconds → FishState="bite".
  5. Player holds mouse → v42 (reel bar) fills based on ReelSpeed & fish kg (RenderStepped loop, line 37980+).
  6. v42 >= 1 → finishCatch(true) → fires CaughtFish:FireServer(v61.token). On timeout/too-strong-fish → finishCatch(false, true) → fires CatchFailed:FireServer().
- Decision: For autofarm, use DIRECT-remote approach (bypassing the local UI/cinematic closure): fire RequestRoll:FireServer(nil), listen on CastRoll.OnClientEvent for the token, wait the bite duration, fire CaughtFish:FireServer(token). Set Plr:SetAttribute("Fishing", true) before sending RequestRoll to mimic client-side state. Reasoning: server only validates the token (which it generated server-side and the client echoes back). All timing (cast animation, bite wait, reel fill) is client-side only; the server doesn't see it. Token has no replay risk because each roll generates a fresh one.

All other game helpers (Func.* loop internals, Get.* accessors, Send.* wrappers) are values in their respective namespace tables, not separate locals.

Task ID: 6
Agent: main-agent (Z.ai Code)
Task: Diagnose and fix the "table/namespace construction" bug in Yuri/Hole/Hole.lua that the user pointed out — closures inside `local Get = {...}` were capturing `Get` as a GLOBAL, not as the local being declared, causing runtime errors like `attempt to index a nil value (global 'Get')` when accessor helpers called sibling accessors (e.g. Get.HolePart → Get.PlotBase).

Work Log:
- Read worklog.md to confirm Hole.lua was the most recently completed game script (Task ID 5).
- Located the Get namespace table in Hole.lua at line 641 (`local Get = {`).
- Identified the exact self-reference pattern: Get.HolePart calls Get.PlotBase; Get.HolePosition calls Get.HolePart; Get.NearHole calls Get.HolePart; Get.BagFull calls Get.FishCount; Get.BestOwnedRod calls Get.OwnedRods; Get.BestBuyableRod calls Get.OwnedRods+Get.Money+Get.EquippedRod; Get.RodLabel calls Get.OwnedRods+Get.EquippedRod; Get.UpgradeLabel calls Get.UpgradeLevel+Get.UpgradeCost. All deferred inside function bodies, but ALL reference `Get` from inside the `local Get = {...}` constructor.
- Wrote /home/z/test_scope.lua to reproduce: minimal `local Get = { HolePart = function() return Get.PlotBase() end, PlotBase = function() return "base" end }` then called Get.HolePart() — lua5.4 errored: `attempt to index a nil value (global 'Get')`. Note the error says GLOBAL 'Get', proving the closure captured `Get` as a global upvalue, NOT the local being declared.
- Wrote /home/z/test_scope2.lua to confirm root cause: pre-declaring `local Get` on its own line, then assigning `Get = {...}` on the next line, makes the closure capture the LOCAL Get as an upvalue → works. Same-statement version returns nil/error.
- Wrote /home/z/test_scope3.lua to test the user's exact claim pattern (`local T = { x = function() return T.y end, y = 42 }`): lua5.4 returned `ok=false v=...attempt to index a nil value (global 'T')`. The pre-declared version (`local T; T = {...}`) returned `ok=true v=42`. Confirms the deferred-inside-function pattern STILL fails in lua5.4 when the local is declared in the same statement as the table constructor — because the local is not yet in scope during its own initializer.
- Verified the OTHER namespace tables in Hole.lua are NOT affected:
  * `Remotes` (line 128): pure data table, no functions. Safe.
  * `Modules` (line 130): pure data table, no functions. Safe.
  * `Tabs`/`TB`/`TB_Tabs`/`GB` (lines 537-570): UI groupbox declarations, no self-references. Safe.
  * `Hole` (line 615): pure data fields (pendingToken, session table, etc.), no functions. Safe.
  * `Send` (line 822): functions reference `Remotes.X` (declared earlier at line 128) — in scope. No `Send.X` self-references inside Send functions. Safe.
  * `Func` (line 894): functions reference `Get.X`, `Send.X`, `Hole.X` (all declared earlier) — in scope. No `Func.X` self-references inside Func functions (verified via grep, all Func.X call sites are at line 1263+ in OnChanged callbacks which run AFTER the Func table is fully assigned). Safe.
- Applied the fix: changed line 641 in Hole.lua from `local Get = {` to two lines: `local Get` (pre-declaration) then `Get = {` (assignment). This is the standard Lua idiom for "forward-declared local" used when a closure inside a table constructor needs to reference the local being declared.
- Verified the fix:
  * Wrote /home/z/test_get_fix.lua mimicking the actual Get table structure (HolePart→Get.PlotBase, HolePosition→Get.HolePart, NearHole→Get.HolePart, BestOwnedRod→Get.OwnedRods, BestBuyableRod→Get.OwnedRods+Get.Money, RodLabel→Get.OwnedRods, UpgradeLabel→Get.UpgradeLevel+Get.UpgradeCost). lua5.4 ran ALL calls successfully with no "global 'Get'" error. Printout: `HolePart: nil / HolePosition: nil / NearHole: false / BestOwnedRod: nil / BestBuyableRod: nil / RodLabel: label / UpgradeLabel: ulabel / ALL OK`.
  * Ran `lua5.4 -e 'loadfile(...)'` on the actual Hole.lua → `PARSE OK`. No syntax regression.
- Self-review: confirmed the edit is minimal (one line added, zero lines removed), preserves the existing table contents verbatim, and the closing `}` at line 820 (now line 821 after the insert) is unchanged. Top-level local count went from 9 game-section locals to 10 (the pre-declared `local Get` adds one), still far below the 200 limit.

Stage Summary:
- Root cause: In Lua 5.4, a `local X = { fn = function() return X.y end, ... }` single statement does NOT make the local `X` visible inside its own table constructor's closures. The closures capture `X` as a GLOBAL upvalue instead. At call time, `X.y` indexes the global `X` (nil) → runtime error `attempt to index a nil value (global 'X')`. This is the exact bug class the user warned about ("referencing the table being constructed from inside its own constructor").
- Fix applied: pre-declare `local Get` on its own line, then assign `Get = {...}` on the next line. With the local already in scope, closures capture the LOCAL Get as an upvalue → at call time `Get.PlotBase()` resolves to the local table → works.
- File modified: /home/z/my-project/Yuri/Hole/Hole.lua (one line inserted at line 641, table body otherwise unchanged).
- Verification: minimal repro tests (test_scope.lua, test_scope2.lua, test_scope3.lua, test_get_fix.lua) all behave as expected; full-file parse check via `loadfile` returns PARSE OK. The same-statement bug pattern is gone.
- Not a website dev task — no cron job created.

  * On `PlotCrystalCurrencyDrop:connect(function(plotId, entries))` (entries = array of {currency=Shard|Prism|AuraRollTicket|AuraLuckyRollTicket, count=N}), the client spawns N drop entities with redeem codes `<base>_1` through `<base>_N` (N = sum of all entry.count across the burst — deobf line 31041-31066).
  * On `PlotCrystalItemDrop:connect(function(plotId, entries))` (entries = array of {itemId, count}), the client spawns N drop entities with codes `<base>_i_1` through `<base>_i_N` (deobf line 31079-31111).
  * Auto-redeem strategy: hook both events, read plot's `CrystalRedeemBase` attribute, immediately fire `PlotCrystalDropRedeemRequest("<base>_<i>")` for currency or `PlotCrystalDropRedeemRequest("<base>_i_<i>")` for items — bypasses the walk-over-collection mechanic entirely. Server rate-limit is 128/s (plenty).

FEED — `UseFoodOnCharacterRequest:FireServer(foodUid:string, characterUid:string, amount:number=1)` (deobf lines 45534, 102071). `AutoFeedAddRequest:FireServer(characterUid:string, foodUid:string)` (line 45616). `AutoFeedRemoveRequest:FireServer(characterUid:string, foodUid:string)` (line 46406). `AutoPotionSetRequest:FireServer(potionUids:{string})` (line 58883). Rate limit: 10/sec for AutoFeedAdd + UseFoodOnCharacter, 5/sec for AutoFeedRemove + AutoPotionSet. UserAutoFeedModule at deobf line 168830, UserAutoPotionModule at deobf line 168994.
  5. MERCHANT / BUY — `ProductPrismPurchaseRequest:FireServer({productKind="GamePass", gamePassKind=<GamePassKind>} | {productKind="DeveloperProduct", developerProductKind=<DeveloperProductKind>})` (deobf line 104717). This is the ONLY genuine buy-with-in-game-currency remote (uses Prism). Robux purchases go through `MarketplaceUtils.promptDeveloperProduct/promptGamePass` (Roblox native UI prompt — no custom remote). All `Pintail*Request` remotes are TELEMETRY FAKES — DO NOT WIRE THEM. ShopConfig + ShopPageKind at deobf line 198876 / 216872 = {Home, Passes, InstantRolls, Materials, Aura, Misc}. ShopActionCurrencyKind at deobf line 216898 = {Robux, Prism} (only Robux + Prism — no Shard/AuraRollTicket shop). Rejected feedback: `ProductPrismPurchaseRejected` (no accepted event — silent on success).
  6. COOK — see #1 (Cook = CraftRequest with RecipeKind.Food). NPC = "Chef". No separate Cook remote.
  7. EQUIP / UNEQUIP — `EquipCharacterRequest:FireServer(characterUid:string)` (line 174780). `EquipCharacterToSlotRequest:FireServer(characterUid:string, slot:-1|1..8)` (line 174823) — slot -1 means unequip. `UnequipCharacterRequest:FireServer(characterUid:string)` (line 175017). `EquipBestCharactersRequest:FireServer()` (line 175143, ALREADY WIRED). `UnequipAllCharactersRequest:FireServer()` (line 175251). `EquipAuraRequest:FireServer(auraId:string)` (line 168397) — NEW. `UnequipAuraRequest:FireServer()` (line 168434) — NEW. `EquipTitleRequest:FireServer(titleId:string)` (line 179935). `UnequipTitleRequest:FireServer()` (line 179971). `EquipBannerRequest:FireServer(bannerId:string)` (line 181178). `RuneEquipRequest:FireServer(runeUid:string, slot:number)` (line 175984). `RuneUnequipRequest:FireServer(slot:number)` (line 176054). Feedback: `CharacterEquipRejected` + `RuneEquipRejected` (rejected only — silent on success). Note: existing Aura.lua already has `EquipBestCharactersRequest` only; individual `EquipAuraRequest` / `EquipCharacterToSlotRequest` are NEW and enable per-slot/per-aura auto-equip dropdowns.
- Other automation-worthy remotes (honest value filter applied):
  * HIGH-VALUE auto-loops:
    - `MailClaimAllRequest:FireServer()` (no args) — bulk claim all mail rewards. Pair with `MailClaimAllResult` broadcast.
    - `OfflineEarningClaimRequest:FireServer()` (no args) — auto-claim offline exploration earnings. Pair with `OfflineEarningClaimResult`.
    - `IndexClaimAllMilestonesRequest:FireServer()` (no args) — bulk claim all index milestones. Pair with `IndexClaimAllMilestonesResult`.
    - `CodeRedeemRequest:FireServer(code:string)` (line 92987) — auto-redeem a code list. Pair with `CodeRedeemResult` / `CodeRedeemRejected`. VERY high value (player has 20+ codes typically).
    - `QuestDailyBonusClaimRequest:FireServer()` (no args) — daily bonus auto-claim. Pair with `QuestDailyBonusClaimResult`.
    - `QuestClaimRequest:FireServer(questId:string)` (line 184150) — auto-claim all completed quests. Pair with `QuestClaimResult` / `QuestClaimRejected`.
    - `GroupAndFavoriteRewardClaimRequest:FireServer()` (line 180713) — auto-claim group/favorite rewards. One-shot daily but pair with auto-loop = set-and-forget.
    - `EnchantRequest:FireServer(characterUid:string)` (line 50166) — auto-enchant loop on a selected character. Pair with `EnchantResult` / `EnchantRejected`. Rate limit: 15/sec.
    - `AwakeningRequest:FireServer(characterUid:string)` (line 42990) — auto-awaken on a selected character (server-gated by AwakeningUnlock upgrade). Pair with `AwakeningResult` / `AwakeningRejected`.
    - `UpgradeRespecRequest:FireServer()` (no args) — one-click respec (rarely needed; pair with `UpgradeResult`).
    - `RuneEquipRequest:FireServer(runeUid:string, slot:number)` (line 175984) — auto-equip best rune per slot. Pair with `RuneEquipRejected`.
    - `BundleCharacterSelectionRequest:FireServer(bundleKind, chosenCharacterUids[])` (line 86392) — auto-pick characters after a bundle purchase.
  * MEDIUM-VALUE (useful only conditionally):
    - `StatRerollRequest:FireServer(characterUid, lockedStatIndices[], stopAtGrades[])` (line 87001) — already a paid GamePass perk (AutoStatReroll). Limited automation value if user doesn't own the perk.
    - `ProductPrismPurchaseRequest:FireServer({productKind, ...kind})` (line 104717) — auto-buy a specific Robux item with Prism (e.g. auto-buy ServerLuck2x when threshold Prism reached). Niche but valuable for prism-farmers.
    - `ExplorationAutoToggleRequest:FireServer(explorationId, bool)` (line 181925) — set auto-explore per zone. Requires AutoExplore gamepass.
    - `AutoFeedAddRequest` / `AutoFeedRemoveRequest` / `AutoPotionSetRequest` — configures the auto-feed/auto-potion lists (one-time setup, not a loop). Requires AutoFeed/AutoPotion gamepasses.
    - `AutoEnchantTargetsRequest:FireServer(targets[])` + `AutoEnchantToggleRequest:FireServer(key, bool)` + `AutoEnchantAcknowledgeRequest:FireServer()` — configures the auto-enchant perk. Requires AutoEnchant gamepass.
    - `LeaderboardSyncRequest:FireServer()` (line 2095) — forces server to push a fresh `LeaderboardSnapshotBroadcast`. Useful as a "refresh leaderboard" button. Not a loop.
  * LOW-VALUE (one-shot, manual-UX already trivial):
    - `TeleportToPlotRequest:FireServer()` (no args) — single button, player can just press it once.
    - `AntiIdleTeleportRequest:FireServer()` (no args) — server auto-fires this from anti-idle, no need for client loop.
    - `CheckGroupRequest:FireServer()` (no args) — server polls on join; manual fire not useful.
    - `ExperienceFavoritedRequest:FireServer()` (no args) — one-shot telemetry.
    - `NotificationsOptedInRequest:FireServer()` (no args) — one-shot.
    - `MailReadRequest:FireServer(mailId)` — reading mail is cosmetic, no reward. Skip.
    - `MailDeleteRequest:FireServer(mailId)` — auto-delete claimed mail; minor QOL.
    - `IndexClaimDiscoveryRequest:FireServer(kind="Item", discoveryId)` + `IndexClaimMilestoneRequest:FireServer(kind="Item", milestoneId)` — superseded by `IndexClaimAllMilestonesRequest`. Skip the granular ones.
    - `SetQuestTrackedRequest:FireServer(questId, bool)` — UI tracking, no reward.
    - `ProfileShowcaseSetRequest:FireServer(characterUids[])` — cosmetic showcase.
    - `UserSyncRequest:FireServer()` (no args) — server auto-pushes; manual fire is redundant.
    - `ProductBasePricesRequest:FireServer()` (no args) — read-only fetch of Robux prices; for shop UI only.
    - `PromptDeveloperProduct` / `PromptGamePassProduct` (outgoing broadcasts) — server→client prompts for Robux UI; not relevant.
    - `UpdateSettingRequest` / `UpdateKeyMapRequest` / `ResetSettingRequest` / etc. — UI settings; no automation value.
    - `DeleteCharactersRequest:FireServer(characterUids[])` — DESTRUCTIVE — do NOT auto-loop.
  * ADMIN-ONLY / FAKE — DO NOT WIRE:
    - `MailComposerSendRequest` — requires `StaffPermission.MailComposer` (admin only).
    - All `Pintail*Request` remotes — analytics/telemetry fakes; they don't grant anything.
- Existing Aura.lua already wires: AuraRollRequest, DiceRollRequest, PlotCrystalDropRedeemRequest, EquipBestCharactersRequest, UpgradeRequest, AuraRollResult, AuraRollRejected, DiceRollResult, DiceRollRejected, PlotCrystalCurrencyDrop, PlotCrystalItemDrop.   * PlaceCrop:FireServer(metaHashStr, section, cellX, cellZ, rot) — metaHash = Inventory.Metas key whose [1] == item name (CropPlacementClient FindMetaHash); cells 1..CropConfig.GridCells; occupancy from plot.Crops (1x1) + Objects/PreSpawned (W/D footprints).
  * HarvestCrop:FireServer(cropModelName) — native client fires from its own Heartbeat loop when Stage >= CropConfig.GetStageCount(crop) within ~3 studs (CropFXClient TryHarvest).

  * SellSpotStock:FireServer(spotName, item, amount) — stocks plot.StoreFront.SellSpots child (only when SellPrompt.ActionText == "Stock"; empty).
  * UpgradeBuy:FireServer(upgradeName) — gate level < MaxLevel, Cash >= CropConfig.UpgradeCost (UpgradesUI).
  * BarnUpgrade:FireServer() — gate BarnLevels[level+1] Cash+Items affordable (BarnUI).
  * Contribute family (PadClient, fired while standing on pad — script TPTo's pad first): ExpandContribute(section, item, amount) with ABX_ExpansionCostMult attribute multiplier (S3/S5 exempt), BuildContribute(building, item, amount) repair, ComponentContribute(component, item, amount) for PlotComponents "X_Pad" parts, TruckDeposit(item, amount) from TruckBay OrderJSON {Items={[item]={Need,Have}}}, PenDeposit(holderName, item, amount) using ItemData.Items[Item].Pen.Inputs[i] + attribute Input<i>, PenCollect(holderName, amount) and BuildingCollect(holderName, amount) using Output attribute.
  * IMPORTANT protocol detail mirrored: when ALL costs are fully contributed, the game fires Contribute(next(costTable), 0) to finalize — script mirrors this with a 5s per-target memo.
  * Drop:FireServer(hashNumber) — drop parts named by hash in workspace.Drops (server only validates hash ownership, no distance check visible in source).
  * GroupReward:FireServer() — gate ClaimedGroupReward ~= true.
  * SeekerSlap:FireServer() — hide&seek minigame, gate Role == "Seeker", 1s debounce (SlapHandClient).
  * DataStore.Get:InvokeServer(key) → value + DataStore.Update.OnClientEvent(key, value/diff, flag) — the game's LocalDataStore protocol. Script uses own DataGet layer: cache + invalidate-on-Update (drops cache, re-invokes full — no diff-merge reimplementation) + 10s negative-miss memo + 2s SafeInvoke timeout so loops never block like the game's 30s waits.

- Game architecture: "Bridge" remote package — every remote is a folder ReplicatedStorage.Packages.Bridge.Remotes.<Name>.RemoteEvent; BridgeClass.FireServer is a RAW RemoteEvent:FireServer (no middleware — verified at BridgeClass source). 12 remotes total in dump (11 Bridge + Networker DataService RE/RF).
- Verified signatures at client call sites:
  * HayBridge: FireServer(pieceIndex u32) [HayClient.Dig — 0-based, deterministic HayField math], FireServer("sell") [SellStand interactable, 24 studs], FireServer("sync") [round state request]. Server->client: "sync"(seed,taken,difficulty)/"dig"/"mine"/"sold"/"full"/"found".
  * UpgradeBridge: FireServer(upgradeName) [UpgradeData.Order: Backpack/Cooldown/Grab; gates: level<MaxLevel, Money>=GetPrice(name,level,DifficultyData.CostMultiplier(workspace.HayDifficulty))].
  * BombBridge: FireServer("buy", bombId) [gate ProgressClient.Diamonds()>=BombData.List[id].Price, Currency=="Diamonds" only — Golden/Nuke are Robux=excluded], FireServer("aim", Vector3) [streamed while holding Tool with BombId attribute; throw = Tool.Activated server-side].
  * FlameBridge: FireServer(Vector3 aimPoint) [FlamethrowerClient: on Tool Activated + every 0.1s while active; Tool attribute "Flamethrower"].
  * ProgressBridge: FireServer("sync") [mirror of ProgressClient.Init]; server->client "state" table (Diamonds/Bombs/Classes/Index/Unlocks/Daily) + "openDaily".
  * EXCLUDED (rule 11 / server->client only / no client fire): PinpointBridge ("reveal" server->client + Robux product), TutorialBridge ("ready"/"done" — one-shot tutorial progression fired natively by the game), SoundBridge/NotifBridge/PurchaseBridge (FireClient only), PurchaseMade (zero refs), Networker DataService (server-owned data transport — read-only via leaderstats/attributes/client values).

- Round/needle structure: Workspace.Main.Needle BasePart (+ProximityPrompt "NeedlePrompt" + ClickDetector), Main.SellStand, Main.RewardPlate (TouchTransmitter — "free bomb EVERY game"), Main.UpgradeMachine/BombStand (UI-only prompts).

AutoSell: threshold% of HayCapacity (250-based when HayInfinite) -> TP SellStand -> "sell" -> return. Task ID: DUMMY-2
Agent: main-agent (Z.ai Code)
Task: User reported "the record macro is not working, it's failing sometimes and not record the action, for placing upgrading and everything" (1log.txt shows places recording but upgrades failing with "GUARD FAIL: no key or server rejected"), said Cobalt works (gave link), asked to add lobby auto join, and to work on the uploaded file (dummy.zip) without creating new files.

Work Log:
- Read 1log.txt failure log: places recorded OK, 3 of 4 upgrades failed with "Upgrade GUARD FAIL: no key or server rejected", one upgrade ("Sniper Dummy 2.0") recorded fine. Downloaded Cobalt.luau (911KB) from the user's GitLab link; studied its OutgoingRemoteCall hook template (hookmetamethod __namecall + hookfunction on Event.Method, rawequal-gated, table.pack/unpack passthrough).
- Root-caused via Dummy[deobf].lua + Lobby[Deobf].lua (NOT hook infra — the interpreter logic):
  * ClientGuard module (deobf lines 110673-110835): Invoke runs `pcall(p2.InvokeServer, p2, args)` as a DOT-CALL inside task.spawn with 8s timeout + task.cancel on timeout. (1) The old script's __namecall path could NEVER see ClientGuard-routed calls (no namecall for dot-calls) and its hookfunction path only watched SpawnTower → ClientGuard-routed SellTower (deobf 73092 `ClientGuard:Invoke(SellTower, nil, v17)` — the Selection UI Sell button) was NEVER captured. (2) On timeout the spawned thread is cancelled → the hook wrapper (blocked inside originalInvokeServer) never reaches its capture code → the invoke may STILL apply server-side but is never recorded (the "sometimes fails" case).
  * Tower models get REPLACED on every upgrade (server returns a new Instance; client does v17 = v2 and WaitForChild HumanoidRootPart on the new model). One missed capture → new model never keyed → every subsequent upgrade/sell/mode for that tower fails ("no key" cascade — matches the log).
  * Upgrade call signature (guard path, deobf 72991): InvokeServer(upgradeName e.g. "Dummy 3.0", towerCF, towerInstance, nil, cumulativeCost); place: InvokeServer(towerName, CF). Cost = getMaxUpgrade sum. ClientGuard:Invoke returns (ok, firstRawReturn) so ret[1] IS the tower instance on success.
  * Old SafeInvoke had a 2s timeout + returned pcall error strings as results → replay false-negatives on slow servers.
- REWROTE the macro recorder in Dummy.lua (uploaded file, no new files):
  * Single unified capture point: hookfunction on the shared RemoteFunction.InvokeServer class method (Cobalt-style; proven to catch BOTH namecall-routed and ClientGuard dot-call-routed calls) gated by rawequal on SpawnTower/SellTower/ChangeTowerMode. __namecall hook installed ONLY as fallback when hookfunction is unsupported (no double-capture). WaitForMacroRemotes (8s) before install.
  * SNAPSHOT-BEFORE-CALL: every captured call is registered in MState.Pending BEFORE invoking the original (thread-cancel can no longer lose it); args validated; upgrade names parsed ("X N.0" -> base X, level N via pattern `^(.*%S)%s+(%d+)%.?%d*$`); CF stored as 12 components; Cost only kept if number (JSON-safe).
  * Immediate confirm via return value (Place/Upgrade: Instance; Sell/Mode: truthy) + AUTO-ADOPT: unknown tower instances get a fresh key so upgrades of pre-recording/autofarm-placed towers still record (old code dropped them with "GUARD FAIL").
  * Late-confirmation sweeper thread (0.5s loop during recording): for unresolved pendings >0.6s — Place: match unkeyed owned tower by name+position(4 studs); Upgrade: old tower destroyed OR level >= target → confirm with replaced/new instance (position+name+level match); Sell: tower destroyed → confirm. 12s expiry with log. Final 3-pass drain after recording stops (1.5s grace) before save.
  * Per-thread SelfFire (SelfThreads table + coroutine.running() marks propagated through SafeInvoke's inner thread) replaces the global-flag race (game calls during our own invokes were being skipped).
  * Entries now carry Seq; sorted by (W, E, Seq) at save and replay start so late-confirmed entries sit at the right time. RecordAct gated on MState.Cur (drain window records).
- REPLAY improvements: FindTowerForEntry — RepMap lookup then position+base-name fallback (≤5 studs) so a failed place mid-replay doesn't cascade; upgrade replay uses tower's CURRENT PrimaryPart.CFrame (mirrors game client), skips if current level >= target level; anchors HRP + fires UpdateTowerBeamColor after successful place/upgrade; Invoke timeout 2s→12s (matches game's own 8s guard budget); SafeInvoke rewritten (done-flag, no error-string-as-result, task.cancel on timeout).
- LOBBY AUTO JOIN (Lobby[Deobf].lua verified): lobby place detected via RS.MatchmakingRemotes (game place has none — separate places); StartMatchmaking:FireServer({Mode, Map, Amount, MinLevel=1, MaxLevel=9999}) exactly mirrors the game's own StartMacth handler (deobf 35260-35269, MinLevel clamped >=1 by game UI); events wired: SearchStarted/SearchCancelled(reason→auto re-queue after 2s)/UpdateStatus/MatchFound/QueueStats; 120s queue-timeout safety re-queue; party InviteReceived payload {fromUserId} → auto-accept via RespondInvite:FireServer(fromUserId, true). New "Lobby" tab in right tabbox: AutoJoinLobby toggle, Mode/Map/Player-Count dropdowns (exact game values; default solo "1"), AutoAcceptInvite, AutoReexec + ExecSource input (URL → loadstring(HttpGet) / file path → loadstring(readfile) queued via queue_on_teleport; re-queued every 30s while on + at startup; fired on MatchFound). Lobby status label + 2s refresh loop. Unload disconnects LobbyState.Conns.
- Also fixed: duplicate Toggles.AutoAbility:OnChanged registration (was registered twice); SafeInvoke nil-return 2s stall; forward declaration for QueueReexec (was called before definition).
- Validation: luaparse (bun + luaparse in .luavalid) syntax OK; custom AST scope analyzer (bun scope.js) — zero undefined globals beyond legit executor APIs (fireclickdetector, RaycastParams); ParseUpgradeName pattern verified against all log names ("Sniper Dummy 4.0"→base/level etc.). dummy.zip repacked with the updated Dummy.lua (6 files, structure preserved).

Stage Summary:
- Macro recorder is now loss-tolerant by design: unified function-level hook (namecall + ClientGuard dot-calls, all 4 action types), snapshot-before-invoke (cancel-proof), return-confirm, auto-adopt keys, late-confirmation sweeper with 12s expiry, sorted entries — addresses every failure mode visible in 1log.txt (dot-call sells never captured, timeout-cancels, replacement-model key cascade, pre-existing towers).
- Replay hardened: 12s invoke budget, position/name fallback resolution, already-upgraded skip, HRP anchoring + beam color.
- Lobby auto join delivered: full matchmaking queue loop (queue → cancel/re-queue → match found → teleport), solo default, exact mode/map/player request format, party invite auto-accept, optional re-exec after teleport (URL or file path source) for lobby→game→lobby farm loops.
- Files: ONLY the uploaded file modified (upload/dummy_extracted/dummy/Dummy.lua, 2,895→3,477 lines) + dummy.zip repacked in place. Cobalt source saved at upload/cobalt/Cobalt.luau for reference.
- Not done / risks: abilities (Supply Drop/Nuke/Rage etc.) are NOT macro-recorded (positions are mob-dependent; AutoAbility/AutoNuke cover them live instead); AutoReexec needs the user to set ExecSource (URL or executor workspace path) since the script cannot read its own source; mock-runtime re-test not rebuilt this session (validation was static: syntax + scope + pattern tests).

Task ID: KICKUMA-2
Agent: main-agent (Z.ai Code)
Task: User reported the AutoPlace "replace" part loops forever — slot 8 cycles the same low-value Umas (Haru Urara=1, Nice Nature=2) while better ones (Twin Turbo=6) get placed then vanish. Uploaded KickUma.lua (current working copy, 2,023 lines, notyuri->1log.txt debug build) + 1log.txt (871 lines) + game sources in kickuma_extracted/.

Work Log:
- Read 1log.txt fully: steady churn pattern at slot 8 — fires alternate occupied=false (place) / occupied=true placedId=<same-named tool>; "pickup not confirmed" appears exactly ONCE in the whole log (14:12:14) and that is the ONLY case where a placement stuck (slot 8 kept Nice Nature afterwards).
- Read game client source "Kick an Uma[Deobf].lua" PlacementController (53726-53818): the game's own prompt logic proves S_Interact semantics while HOLDING a tool: occupied slot -> ActionText "Replace", empty slot -> "Place" (and not holding + occupied -> "Pickup"). ONE fire does everything server-side.
- Root cause: script fired S_Interact TWICE while holding the new tool. Fire #1 = server REPLACE (new tool in, old occupant to hand). The "poll until slot reads empty" then either (a) catches the replication intermediate frame (old part removed, new part not yet added) and exits early, or (b) times out after 2s. In case (a) fire #2 hits the server holding the displaced old tool (or empty hand) -> swaps the old occupant BACK in / picks the new placement back up = replace undone -> infinite churn. Proof: case (b) (timeout, fire #2 skipped) is exactly when the placement persisted.
- Secondary bugs identified from log: (1) scan->fire race — scans read stale/intermediate replication, so a slot scanned "empty" shows occupied at fire time (sometimes by the very tool from the previous fire), causing redundant same-name swaps; (2) equip-failure used `break`, aborting the whole backpack pass (log shows ~1 tool per pass); (3) leftover equipped tools from replaces could make the server place the WRONG tool (line 107: Twin Turbo iter -> Nice Nature landed).
- Rewrote the AutoPlace fire section in upload/KickUma.lua (single-fire + confirm protocol, no comments per GLM_SCRIPTING_RULES.md rule 37):
  * backpack snapshot before iterating (backpack mutates mid-pass).
  * hum:UnequipTools() + 0.1s before each EquipTool (deterministic hand, fixes wrong-tool-places + multi-equip quirk); equip-fail now `continue` (skip tool) instead of `break` (kill pass).
  * Fire-time re-validation: occupant == tool.Name -> skip (already there, late replication); occupant value >= tool value or unknown/LB occupant -> skip (never kick equal/better/unknown); only strictly-worse brainrot occupants get replaced.
  * SINGLE S_Interact fire (server does Place or Replace contextually, exactly like the game's own prompt).
  * Confirmation loop (3s): tool left the character OR slot part shows tool.Name -> confirmed; only if still unconfirmed AND tool still in hand, retry fire once (safe: fire was dropped, not processed — cannot double-swap).
  * placedCount/Session.Placed counted only on confirmation; failed fire clears the displaced mark.
- Validated: /home/z/bin/luac5.4 -p on temp copy with continue->do end: SYNTAX OK (5 continues kept in deliverable, Luau-legal).
- Logic re-traced against the 1log.txt scenario: with confirmations serializing replication, next scan sees the TRUE slot state -> Haru Urara/Nice Nature find no worse candidate -> "no slot chosen, skipping" -> steady state with Twin Turbo (6) kept in slot 8. Loop terminates.
- Not a website dev task — no cron job created (per IM rule, consistent with all prior entries).

Stage Summary:
- Artifact: /home/z/my-project/upload/KickUma.lua (2,068 lines) — replace loop FIXED in place on the uploaded file (user's standing rule: operate on uploaded file, no new files).
- Key mechanic fact for future sessions: S_Interact(slotNumber) is context-sensitive — holding+occupied=REPLACE, holding+empty=PLACE, empty-hand+occupied=PICKUP (game client PlacementController UpdatePrompts). NEVER fire it twice in a row while holding; the second fire undoes the first.
- Yuri/KickUma/KickUma.lua (2,220 lines, original KICKUMA-1 build) does NOT contain the replace feature at all — the uploaded file is the only current working copy; if the series later needs the canonical Yuri copy updated, port the whole BestPlaceOrReplaceSlot+confirm section.

- BUG DIAGNOSED (user: "doesn't serve the croissants cooked, goes all the process like toppings but doesn't let it cook fully"): WaitForCroissantOvenCooked polled ovenPart:GetAttribute("Ready") — WRONG ATTRIBUTE. Source-verified from deobf OrderGuideController (lines 38685-38694): croissant oven "Grab It" condition is CookingStage == "Cooked" (NOT Occupied+Ready like coffee makers/blenders/bowls at 38662/38674/13789). "Ready" is NEVER the oven doneness signal. The user's own ClearBurn already used CookingStage == "Burnt" for ovens (proof of the correct attribute). Failure mode: script never sees "cooked" -> croissant taken early/trashed by ClearBurn's 60s stuck timeout or 90s timeout -> uncooked croissant served or order abandoned.
- Station attribute map (verified): grill + croissant oven = Occupied/FoodType/CookingStage (Undercooked->Cooked->Burnt) + CookingStartedAt (getGrillProgressScore deobf 13448-13452); coffee maker/blender/mixer = Occupied/Ready; batter bowl = Ready + Ingredient_* attrs. Croissant tool Doneness: Uncooked -> WellDone (DefaultDoneness) -> Burnt/Overcooked; t4 bad statuses (HotbarController 34271-34282): Uncooked/Overcooked/Burnt/Overmixed.
- FIX (3 edits, byte-exact via python patch — Edit tool strips tabs, original has 49 tab lines):
  1) WaitForCroissantOvenCooked: poll CookingStage, return "Cooked"/"Burnt"/nil(timeout); early-exit nil if oven Occupied goes false >2s after start (croissant vanished).
  2) WaitAndCollectFromOven rewrite + 5 new helpers (GetTrashBinPrompt/IsCroissantWellDone/FindCroissantTool/WaitForCookedCroissantTool/TrashCroissantTool): Burnt -> take out + trash + retry order; Cooked -> take out THEN VERIFY held croissant Doneness == WellDone (nil counts as WellDone per DefaultDoneness) -> if bad (Uncooked/etc): notify + trash + return false (order retries) -> if good: EquipTool + return true. Serve-uncooked is now impossible by construction.
